- `s6` [hiring] 2NS's careers page says it is hiring, with open roles in offensive security, AllSecure, security management and architecture, sales, and a partner network of freelancers.  
  > "Katso avoimet työpaikkamme rekryportaalistamme." ([source](https://www.2ns.fi/toihin-meille/))
- `s7` [product, 2026] 2NS offers AllSecure, a cybersecurity situational awareness and automated security monitoring product.  
  > "2NS AllSecure – Cybersecurity Situational Awareness" ([source](https://www.2ns.fi/en/latest-news/))

Growing Finnish security firm winning NATO supplier status and government contracts while scaling its AI security services.

- **Cloud and identity security architects** (Senior; Microsoft Azure, Entra ID, Microsoft 365, Identity and access management, Cloud security): Its open Senior Security Architect role asks for Azure, Entra ID and Microsoft 365 security design across customer environments. (s6, hiring now: Senior Security Architect – Microsoft & Cloud Security)

- `s2` [product, 2026-03] Chipmetrics launched a pre-patterned embedded silicon test structure allowing electrical characterization of thin films immediately after deposition, without lithography or etching.  
  > "The structure enables immediate electrical measurements directly after film deposition" ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/chipmetrics-introduces-pre-patterned-electrical-test-structure-for-rapid-thin-film-characterization/))
- `s3` [customers, 2026-02] Chipmetrics is targeting entry into mass-production lines of Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix in South Korea, and says its measurement method is hundreds of times faster than cross-section TEM.  
  > "Chipmetrics is aiming to enter the mass-production lines of Samsung Electronics and SK Hynix" ([source](https://www.digitaltoday.co.kr/en/view/3830/chipmetrics-targets-south-korea-as-3d-nand-heads-toward-1000-layers))
- `s4` [growth, 2025-12] Chipmetrics reported record sales for 2025, describing it as its key achievement of the year.  
  > "The company reached record sales." ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/chipmetrics-2025-a-year-of-momentum-discovery-and-record-growth/))
- `s5` [organisation, 2025-01] Chipmetrics set up a Dresden-based subsidiary in January 2025.  
  > "Chipmetrics Establishes Dresden-Based Subsidiary" ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/news/))
- `s6` [funding, 2024-11] Chipmetrics received a 1 million euro award from Business Finland's Young Innovative Company funding programme, to be used for business development and international expansion.  
  > "awarded 1 million euro from Business Finland’s Young Innovative Company (YIC) funding programi" ([source](https://www.blog.baldengineering.com/2024/11/chipmetrics-secures-1-million-from.html))
- `s7` [funding, 2022-09] Chipmetrics closed a 340,000 euro seed round led by Redstone's North Karelia Growth Fund.  
  > "announced it has closed its 340.000 euro seed round" ([source](https://www.blog.baldengineering.com/2022/09/chipmetrics-announces-completion-of.html))
- `s8` [partnership, 2026-02] Chipmetrics and Park Systems are collaborating to combine Chipmetrics test chips with Park Systems scanning probe microscopy platforms for semiconductor metrology.  
  > "By combining Chipmetrics’ standardized test chips with Park Systems’ industry-leading scanning probe microscopy (SPM) platforms" ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/chipmetrics-and-park-systems-enabling-the-next-generation-of-semiconductor-metrology/))
- `s9` [technology, 2026-07] Chipmetrics and Aalto University presented a combined conformality and electrical characterization of platinum ALD on a single test structure at ALD/ALE 2026.  
  > "Together with Aalto University, we showed what we believe is the first combined conformality and electrical characterization of Pt ALD" ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/ald-conformality-ald-ale-2026/))
- `s10` [hiring] Chipmetrics' careers page lists a general open-position application form without naming specific roles.  
  > "Fill in the open position application form and we will contact you." ([source](https://chipmetrics.com/careers/))

Chipmetrics is launching multi-material test chips and targeting entry into Samsung and SK Hynix mass-production lines.

- **Semiconductor process and metrology engineers** (Experienced; Thin-film deposition, ALD, Electrical characterization, Cross-section TEM, ISO 9001): Chipmetrics is pursuing entry into Samsung and SK Hynix mass-production lines, which requires qualifying its measurement method on production processes.   - can do: Support yield optimization for 3D NAND customers
- **Scanning probe microscopy specialists** (Experienced; Scanning probe microscopy, Semiconductor metrology, Data analysis, Python): Chipmetrics announced a collaboration with Park Systems to combine its test chips with scanning probe microscopy platforms for semiconductor metrology. (s8)
  - can do: Operate scanning probe microscopy platforms on semiconductor test chips
  - can do: Integrate SPM measurements with electrical characterization workflows
  - can do: Validate measurement repeatability across Park Systems platforms
- **Business development managers** (Experienced; Semiconductor sales, Key account management, Korean market entry, Technical sales): Chipmetrics received 1 million euros for business development and international expansion, and set up a Dresden subsidiary in 2025. (s6, s5, s3)
  - can do: Open accounts with memory and packaging manufacturers in Korea and Europe
  - can do: Manage technical sales cycles from R&D to mass production
